I modified `kilo.json` but `kilo.jsonc` is separate.\n \nSo the OAuth token won't work directly as an API key since Anthropic expects it in an `Authorization: Bearer` header, but the SDK sends it as `x-api-key`. Without an API key, the SDK would either skip setting the header entirely or pass an undefined value, both of which would fail.
